### eic.self_employed — Step 5, question 2: self-employed (Worksheet B)
Status: **DISAGREE**

Project:

```json
{"args": [{"const": 0, "type": "Count"}, {"collection": "person", "field": "person.sc_count", "op": "sum_over", "where": null}], "test": "lt"}
```

Second:

```json
{"expression": {"args": [{"args": [{"const": 0, "type": "Count"}, {"collection": "person", "field": "person.sc_count", "op": "sum_over", "where": null}], "test": "lt"}, {"args": [{"const": 0}, {"collection": "person", "field": "person.eic_se_has", "op": "sum_over", "where": null}], "test": "lt"}], "test": "any"}, "invalid_model_types": [{"line": "eic.self_employed", "message": "malformed expression (project schema): data/any/0 must be object, data/any/0 must be object, data/any/0 must be object"}, {"line": "eic.self_employed", "message": "eic.eic.self_employed: malformed expression"}]}
```

Ruling: model error; on the declaration: The reply fails the project schema (eic.self_employed: "data/any/0 must be object"; eic.separated_box: "data/all/0 must be object": their any/all lists name helper ids instead of tests), so triage did not execute it (triage/eic.deepseek.json state invalid) and every line is ruled by reading the expressions against the source (README). On the reading as well: i1040gi p. 42, Step 5, question 2: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee? Yes. Skip question 3 and Step 6; go to Worksheet B." The candidate's test is any person's Worksheet B self-employment amount nonzero (person.eic_se_has) or any Schedule C (person.sc_count); the project's is a Schedule C (eic.sc_count > 0), clergy, church employee and statutory employee income being refused by scope.schedule1. A Schedule C with a loss is self-employment either way, so the second disjunct is the project's; the first adds nothing the second does not hold, as person.eic_se_has is figured from the person's Schedules C (person.sc_total, or the Schedule SE Part 1 amount when Schedule SE is filed, which in this tool requires a Schedule C). The ruling adjudicates the declaration, not a tax difference.

Ruling: model error; The reply fails the project schema (eic.self_employed: "data/any/0 must be object"; eic.separated_box: "data/all/0 must be object": their any/all lists name helper ids instead of tests), so triage did not execute it (triage/eic.deepseek.json state invalid) and every line is ruled by reading the expressions against the source (README). The reply has no Steps 1 to 5 at all: no Step 1 income limit or valid-SSN test (p. 40, Step 1, questions 1 and 2), no investment income test (p. 40, Step 2, question 2: "Is your investment income more than $11,950?"), no Step 3 or Step 4 test (p. 42, Step 4, questions 1 to 6, the age, main-home and dependent tests) and no Step 5, question 3 earned-income limit; Worksheet A and B are figured for every filer from the EIC Table alone. i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 2: "Do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have a valid SSN? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." The candidate gives the table credit to, for example, a filer without a valid SSN, a filer with $20,000 of investment income, or a filer with no qualifying child under age 25 or 65 or older (p. 42, Step 4, question 2), all of whom the source stops. It also routes on its invalid eic.self_employed. Its only refusal is household.credit_disallowed (Form 8862), so the Step 1 and Step 2 scope screens the project carries are absent. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: The closed interface has no separate 'do not want to claim' election; the box is modeled as checked when the computed EIC is zero.

Ruling: model error; The reply fails the project schema (eic.self_employed: "data/any/0 must be object"; eic.separated_box: "data/all/0 must be object": their any/all lists name helper ids instead of tests), so triage did not execute it (triage/eic.deepseek.json state invalid) and every line is ruled by reading the expressions against the source (README). i1040gi p. 43, Line 27c: "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a." The candidate checks the box whenever its credit is zero, so it checks it after STOPs that give no 27c instruction (p. 40, Step 1, question 1; Step 2; p. 42, Step 4, question 2) and for a filer who simply gets no credit, as ruled in round 4 (classm.f2441.2.deepseek: Step 1, question 1 STOP, project no). The instructed cases (Step 1, question 2; Step 3, question 6; Step 4, questions 3 and 5; Worksheet B line 4b and line 5; Worksheet A line 2 zero) are the project's eic.check_27c terms. Project stands.

### eic.separated_box — A married filer filing separately or as head of household who passes Step 3, questions 4 a

Ruling: model error; The reply fails the project schema (eic.self_employed: "data/any/0 must be object"; eic.separated_box: "data/all/0 must be object": their any/all lists name helper ids instead of tests), so triage did not execute it (triage/eic.deepseek.json state invalid) and every line is ruled by reading the expressions against the source (README). i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 2: "If line 2 is zero, STOP You can't take the credit." The candidate looks line 2 up on every return, after any STOP, and without a guard for amounts outside the table (no row below $1, none at or above the limit), where the project's table lookup has no band; The reply has no Steps 1 to 5 at all: no Step 1 income limit or valid-SSN test (p. 40, Step 1, questions 1 and 2), no investment income test (p. 40, Step 2, question 2: "Is your investment income more than $11,950?"), no Step 3 or Step 4 test (p. 42, Step 4, questions 1 to 6, the age, main-home and dependent tests) and no Step 5, question 3 earned-income limit; Worksheet A and B are figured for every filer from the EIC Table alone. Project stands.

Ruling: model error; The reply fails the project schema (eic.self_employed: "data/any/0 must be object"; eic.separated_box: "data/all/0 must be object": their any/all lists name helper ids instead of tests), so triage did not execute it (triage/eic.deepseek.json state invalid) and every line is ruled by reading the expressions against the source (README). i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 6: "This is your earned income credit." The candidate's line 6 (line 2 when line 4 is Yes or line 3 is below the line 5 amount, the smaller of lines 2 and 5 otherwise) is the project's choice, but figured and shown on every return, after any STOP; The reply has no Steps 1 to 5 at all: no Step 1 income limit or valid-SSN test (p. 40, Step 1, questions 1 and 2), no investment income test (p. 40, Step 2, question 2: "Is your investment income more than $11,950?"), no Step 3 or Step 4 test (p. 42, Step 4, questions 1 to 6, the age, main-home and dependent tests) and no Step 5, question 3 earned-income limit; Worksheet A and B are figured for every filer from the EIC Table alone. Project stands.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | household.credit_disallowed | - |
| eic.check_27c | household.credit_disallowed | household.credit_disallowed |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: f1040.line1z
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: eic.h_zero
    note: "The closed input interface provides no Medicaid waiver payment amount; within the declared scope the line is entered as zero."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: subtract
      from: eic.step5_line1
      take: eic.step5_line2
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: eic.h_zero
    note: "The closed input interface provides no nontaxable combat pay amount or election; within the declared scope the line is entered as zero."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of:
        - eic.step5_line3
        - eic.step5_line4
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: sum
      of:
        - eic.earned_income
        - eic.h_se_total
    note: "Lines 1e, 2c, and 3 are represented by the supplied per-person self-employment earned-income interface; the combined amount is modeled as Step 5 earned income plus the per-person self-employment amount."
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: subtract_floor0
      from: eic.wsb_4b
      take:
        const: 0
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: eic.earned_income
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            ge:
              - eic.h_qc_count
              - const: 3
                type: Count
          then: eic.h_table3
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq:
              - eic.h_qc_count
              - const: 2
                type: Count
          then: eic.h_table2
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq:
              - eic.h_qc_count
              - const: 1
                type: Count
          then: eic.h_table1
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq:
              - eic.h_qc_count
              - const: 0
                type: Count
          then: eic.h_table0
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: copy
      from: f1040.line11b
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      eq:
        - eic.line3
        - eic.line1
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq:
              - eic.h_below5
              - const: true
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            eq:
              - eic.h_below5
              - const: false
          then: eic.h_line5_lookup
    note: "When the instructions say to leave line 5 blank, this model stores zero and line 6 uses line 2 as instructed."
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - op: test
                eq:
                  - eic.line4
                  - const: true
              - op: test
                eq:
                  - eic.h_below5
                  - const: true
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            not:
              op: test
              any:

                  eq:

                    - const: true
                - op: test
                  eq:

                    - const: true

  - id: eic.separated_box

    printed: "(helper)"

    printed: "(helper)"

      eq:

        - eic.wsb_6
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line11_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:

        - eic.h_wsb_line10_lookup
  - id: eic.h_wsb_credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"

    expr:

    printed: "(helper)"

  - id: eic.h_sep_status
    printed: "(helper)"

            - const: "hoh"
  - id: eic.h_sep_condition
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          eq:

            - const: false
        - op: test
          eq:
            - eic.legally_separated
            - const: true
  - id: eic.h_qc_at_least1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      ge:
        - eic.h_qc_count
        - const: 1
          type: Count
  - id: eic.h_qc_not_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "(helper)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      eq:
        - eic.qc_of_another
        - const: false
```
